 m=N//2; t=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m

   if a<1e-6: x=0.5
   else:
     c=(a-1)/(2*a)

def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=n/2
 for _ in range(200):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
      if tot<1e-15: break
      h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*room,0,1)
   else:
      tot=h.sum(); ...
 return h
```

```
def exact_sum_fix(h,n):
 target=n/2
 for _ in range(5):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.argmax(1-h)
      h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
   else:
      idx=np.argmax(h)
      h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
 return h
```
This may move one point by up to diff if room enough; if not? Room maybe not enough if all near1 and diff large. But after projection diff small. For half target m/2.

But if using midpoint analytic, sum may be off by O(1/n) maybe <1e-3; exact fix okay.

 return h,c

def fix_sum(h,target):
 for _ in range(10):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
     idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
     add=min(h[idx]? no if diff >1-h[idx], use distribute)
     # if diff <=1-h[idx], set; else add max and continue
     h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
   else:
     idx=int(np.argmax(h))
     h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If diff large > room max, one adjustment not enough; loop handles. But if all at1 and diff>0 impossible (target > n). target n/2, average .5, room enough. Good.

def poly_half(N,a,kind):
 m=N//2
 t=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
 if kind==1:
   c=(a-1.0)/(2.0*a)
   r=1.0-c
   x=c+r*np.power(t,a)
 else:
   c=(1.0-a)/2.0
   r=1.0-c
   x=1.0-r*np.power(1.0-t,a)
 return project_half(x,m)
```

`compute_c5_full`:
```
def compute_c5_full(h):
 n=len(h)
 c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
 return float(np.max(c)*2.0/n)
```

`project_feasible`: use target n/2.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
 h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
 target=0.5*n
 for _ in range(100):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   if diff>0:
     room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room,0,1)
   else:
     tot=h.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h=np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*h,0,1)
 return fix_sum(h,target)
```
`fix_sum(h,target)` define before? Top-level order doesn't matter if called after definition at runtime. But functions can call later defined if at call time. Fine. Or define fix first.

`project_half` analogous target+m/2 fix.

`fix_sum`:
```
def fix_sum(h,target):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(10):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: return h
   if diff>0:
     idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
     add=min(1.0-h[idx], diff)
   else:
     idx=int(np.argmax(h))
     add=max(-h[idx], diff) # diff negative
   h[idx]+=add
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 return h
```
If diff large and add saturates, loop continues. Good.

   improved=False
   if rng is not None:
      order=rng.permutation(n)
   else:
      order=np.arange(n)
   for p in order:
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     xnew=solve_coord_fast(cvec,h,p,n,nbisect)

   if not improved:
      break

   h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   newc=compute_c5_full(h)
   if newc < best_c - 1e-15:
      best_c=newc
   elif newc > best_c + 1e-12:
      # sym/projection increased; break? But sym should not if h before? maybe due projection. Keep best? We don't store best_h. Could store.

`resample_half(h, N)`:
```
def resample_half(h,N):
 if len(h)==N:
   h=0.5*(h+h[::-1]); h=project_feasible(h,N)
   return project_half(h[:N//2],N//2)
 xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h))
 xn=(np.arange(N)+0.5)*(2.0/N)
 hi=np.interp(xn,xo,h)
 hi=0.5*(hi+hi[::-1]); hi=project_feasible(hi,N)
 return project_half(hi[:N//2],N//2)
```

Pseudo:
```
def solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,nbisect=35):
 h0=h[p]
 if h0<1e-14 or h0>1-1e-14: return h0
 # affected ms
 lo_a=max(0,n-1-p); hi_a=min(2*n-2,n-1+p) ???
```
Need derive ms ranges correctly. Previous code:
```
lo_a = max(0, n - 1 - p)
hi_a = min(2*n - 2, n - 1 + p)
lo_b = max(0, p)
hi_b = min(2*n - 2, p + n - 1)
m_lo=min(lo_a,lo_b); m_hi=max(hi_a,hi_b)
ms=np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
jf=p+ms-(n-1); jb=p-ms+(n-1)
vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
a=zeros; if vf: a[vf]+=1-h[jf]; if vb: a[vb]-=h[jb]
```
This identifies coefficients where p appears as first (jf valid) or second (jb valid). We trust.
Then c_base = c[ms].copy.
Diag idx where ms == n-1? For lag0 coefficient, p appears as both first and second? Our a vector would add (1-h[p]) and subtract h[p] =1-2h0 at lag0, but actual quadratic. Previous sets diag_idx and zeroes a, c_diag. Good.
For has_diag, c_diag old = c_base[diag]. For non_diag, linear coeffs a_nd, constants c_nd.
Affected coefficient as function of x:
- non_diag: value = c_nd + a*x (no quadratic)
- diag: value = c_diag + (x - h0) - (x^2 - h0^2) ? Actually old term h0(1-h0). New x(1-x). Difference = x(1-x)-h0(1-h0). So value = c_diag - h0(1-h0) + x(1-x). Previous `w = T - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0`? Since x(1-x) = x -x^2. Condition value ≤T => x -x^2 ≤ T - c_diag + h0(1-h0) ? h0(1-h0)=h0-h0^2. Yes Cq=T - c_diag + h0 - h0^2. Good.
Feasibility for T:
1. Unaffected max > T? infeasible.
2. Affine constraints: for each non_diag, c_nd + a*x ≤T.
   If a<0: x ≥ (T-c_nd)/a (since divide negative) => lower bound.
   If a>0: x ≤ (T-c_nd)/a.
   If a≈0: require c_nd≤T.
   Intersect with [0,1].
3. Diagonal constraint: x(1-x) ≤ Cq.
   Intersect linear interval with feasible set.

  xl=0.0; xh=1.0

  pos=a_nd > 1e-13

```
import numpy as np
import time

def fix_sum(h, target):
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            return np.clip(h,0,1)
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            add = max(-h[idx], diff)
        h[idx] += add
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If diff negative and h[idx]=0 but diff large? max(-0,diff)=diff, h becomes negative if diff<-h? Wait if diff negative, we need decrease; add=diff - min? Code `add = max(-h[idx], diff)`: if h=0.1, diff=-0.2, -h=-0.1, max=-0.1, decreases to0. Loop continues. Good. If diff positive, add min(room,diff). Good.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=0.5*n
 for _ in range(120):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
     room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h=np.clip(h + diff/tot*room,0,1)
   else:
     tot=h.sum()
     if tot<1e-15: break
     h=np.clip(h + diff/tot*h,0,1)
 return fix_sum(h,target)
```

```
def solve_coord_fast(c,h,p,n,nbisect=35):
 h0=float(h[p])
 if h0 < 1e-14 or h0 > 1.0-1e-14: return h0
 lo_a = max(0, n - 1 - p)
 hi_a = min(2*n - 2, n - 1 + p)
 lo_b = max(0, p)
 hi_b = min(2*n - 2, p + n - 1)
 m_lo = min(lo_a, lo_b); m_hi = max(hi_a, hi_b)
 ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi + 1, dtype=int)
 jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
 jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
 vf = (jf >=0) & (jf < n)
 vb = (jb >=0) & (jb < n)
 a = np.zeros(len(ms))
 if np.any(vf): a[vf] += (1.0 - h[jf[vf]])
 if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
 c_base = c[ms].copy()
 diag = np.where(ms == (n-1))[0]
 has_diag = diag.size > 0
 c_diag=0.0
 if has_diag:
   c_diag = float(c_base[diag[0]])
   a[diag] = 0.0
   nd = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool); nd[diag]=False
 else:
   nd = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
 a_nd = a[nd]; c_nd = c_base[nd]
 unaff = 0.0
 if m_lo > 0: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:m_lo])))
 if m_hi < 2*n - 2: unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[m_hi+1:])))
 L_lo = unaff
 L_hi = float(np.max(c))
 for _ in range(nbisect):
   T = (L_lo + L_hi)*0.5
   feasible = True
   if unaff > T + 1e-15: feasible=False
   if feasible and a_nd.size:
      xl=0.0; xh=1.0
      neg = a_nd < -1e-13; pos = a_nd > 1e-13; zero = ~(neg|pos)
      if zero.size and np.any(c_nd[zero] > T + 1e-14): feasible=False
      if feasible and neg.size: xl = max(xl, float(np.max((T - c_nd[neg]) / a_nd[neg])))
      if feasible and pos.size: xh = min(xh, float(np.min((T - c_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos])))
      xl = max(xl,0.0); xh = min(xh,1.0)
      if feasible and xl > xh + 1e-14: feasible=False
   else:
      xl=0.0; xh=1.0
   if feasible and has_diag:
      Cq = T - c_diag + h0 - h0*h0
      if Cq < -1e-13: feasible=False
      elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
         disc = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
         if disc < 0.0: disc = 0.0
         rt = np.sqrt(disc)
         r1 = (1.0 - rt)*0.5
         r2 = (1.0 + rt)*0.5
         low_ok = max(xl, 0.0) <= min(xh, r1) + 1e-13
         high_ok = max(xl, r2) <= min(xh, 1.0) + 1e-13
         if not (low_ok or high_ok): feasible=False
   if feasible: L_hi = T
   else: L_lo = T
 T = (L_lo + L_hi)*0.5
 # compute final xl,xh
 xl=0.0; xh=1.0
 if a_nd.size:
   neg=...
   ...
 if has_diag:
   Cq=...
   if Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
      ... r1,r2
      if Cq < -1e-13:
         # choose h0? But T from feasible should not.
         x = h0
      else:
         mid = 0.5*(xl+xh)
         # if mid in forbidden
         if mid > r1 + 1e-12 and mid < r2 - 1e-12:
            # choose closer of r1,r2 within [xl,xh]
            d1=abs(mid-r1); d2=abs(mid-r2)
            if d1 <= d2:
               x = min(max(r1, xl), xh)
            else:
               x = min(max(r2, xl), xh)
         else:
            x = mid
   else:
      x=0.5*(xl+xh)
 else:
   x=0.5*(xl+xh)
 if xl > xh + 1e-10: return h0
 return float(np.clip(x,0,1))
```

Now coordinate descent with h_best:
```
def coord_descent(h,n,max_passes,deadline,rng=None,nbisect=35):
 h=project_feasible(h,n)
 h_best=h.copy()
 c_best=compute_c5_full(h)
 for _ in range(max_passes):
   if time.time() > deadline: break
   cvec=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
   improved=False
   if rng is not None:
      order = rng.permutation(n)
   else: order=np.arange(n)
   for p in order:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      xnew=solve_coord_fast(cvec,h,p,n,nbisect)
      if abs(xnew - h[p]) > 1e-12:
         h[p]=xnew; improved=True
   if not improved:
      break
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   h=0.5*(h+h[::-1])
   h=project_feasible(h,n)
   newc=compute_c5_full(h)
   if newc < c_best - 1e-16:
      c_best=newc; h_best=h.copy()
   # if not improved, break
   if newc >= c_best - 1e-16:
      break
   h=h_best.copy()
 return h_best,c_best
```
This reverts any pass that doesn't increase best by storing h_best. But note if pass improved, h is updated; next pass starts from h_best. If pass not improved, break. Good.
Potential issue: even if newc == c_best but h changed, could be new plateau allowing future improvement; but break okay.

def poly_half(N,a,kind):
 m=N//2
 t=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/float(m)
 if kind == 1:
   c=(a-1.0)/(2.0*a)
   r=1.0-c
   x=c + r*np.power(t,a)
 else:
   c=(1.0-a)/2.0
   r=1.0-c
   x=1.0 - r*np.power(1.0-t,a)
 return project_half(x,m)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def fix_sum(v, target):
    v = np.clip(np.array(v, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(24):
        diff = target - float(v.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            return np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - v))
            add = min(1.0 - v[idx], diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(v))
            add = max(-v[idx], diff)
        v[idx] += add
    return np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)


def proj_sum(v, target):
    v = np.array(v, dtype=float).copy()
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - float(v.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - v
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            v = np.clip(v + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(v.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            v = np.clip(v + (diff / tot) * v, 0.0, 1.0)
    return fix_sum(v, target)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    return proj_sum(h, 0.5 * n)


def project_half(x, m):
    return proj_sum(x, 0.5 * m)


def make_full(x, n=None):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if n is None:
        n = 2 * len(x)
    if n == 2 * len(x):
        return np.concatenate((x, x[::-1]))
    old = np.concatenate((x, x[::-1]))
    to = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(old))
    tn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return project_feasible(np.interp(tn, to, old), n)


def compute_c5_full(h):
    n = len(h)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def compute_c5_half(x, n):
    return compute_c5_full(make_full(x, n))


def solve_coord_fast(c, h, p, n, nbisect=35):
    h0 = float(h[p])
    if h0 < 1e-14 or h0 > 1.0 - 1e-14:
        return h0

    lo_a = max(0, n - 1 - p)
    hi_a = min(2 * n - 2, n - 1 + p)
    lo_b = max(0, p)
    hi_b = min(2 * n - 2, p + n - 1)
    m_lo = min(lo_a, lo_b)
    m_hi = max(hi_a, hi_b)
    ms = np.arange(m_lo, m_hi + 1, dtype=int)

    jf = p + ms - (n - 1)
    jb = p - ms + (n - 1)
    vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
    vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)

    a = np.zeros(len(ms))
    if np.any(vf):
        a[vf] += (1.0 - h[jf[vf]])
    if np.any(vb):
        a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]

    c_base = c[ms].copy()
    diag = np.where(ms == (n - 1))[0]
    has_diag = diag.size > 0
    c_diag = 0.0
    if has_diag:
        c_diag = float(c_base[diag[0]])
        a[diag] = 0.0
        nd = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)
        nd[diag] = False
    else:
        nd = np.ones(len(ms), dtype=bool)

    a_nd = a[nd]
    c_nd = c_base[nd]

    unaff = 0.0
    if m_lo > 0:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:m_lo])))
    if m_hi < 2 * n - 2:
        unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[m_hi + 1:])))

    L_lo = unaff
    L_hi = float(np.max(c))

    for _ in range(nbisect):
        T = 0.5 * (L_lo + L_hi)
        feasible = True
        if unaff > T + 1e-15:
            feasible = False

        if feasible and a_nd.size:
            xl = 0.0
            xh = 1.0
            neg = a_nd < -1e-13
            pos = a_nd > 1e-13
            zero = ~(neg | pos)
            if zero.size and np.any(c_nd[zero] > T + 1e-14):
                feasible = False
            if feasible and neg.size:
                xl = max(xl, float(np.max((T - c_nd[neg]) / a_nd[neg])))
            if feasible and pos.size:
                xh = min(xh, float(np.min((T - c_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos])))
            xl = max(xl, 0.0)
            xh = min(xh, 1.0)
            if feasible and xl > xh + 1e-14:
                feasible = False
        else:
            xl = 0.0
            xh = 1.0

        if feasible and has_diag:
            Cq = T - c_diag + h0 - h0 * h0
            if Cq < -1e-13:
                feasible = False
            elif Cq < 0.25 - 1e-13:
                disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
                rt = np.sqrt(disc)
                r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
                r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
                low_ok = max(xl, 0.0) <= min(xh, r1) + 1e-13
                high_ok = max(xl, r2) <= min(xh, 1.0) + 1e-13
                if not (low_ok or high_ok):
                    feasible = False

        if feasible:
            L_hi = T
        else:
            L_lo = T

    T = 0.5 * (L_lo + L_hi)
    xl = 0.0
    xh = 1.0
    if a_nd.size:
        neg = a_nd < -1e-13
        pos = a_nd > 1e-13
        zero = ~(neg | pos)
        if neg.size:
            xl = max(xl, float(np.max((T - c_nd[neg]) / a_nd[neg])))
        if pos.size:
            xh = min(xh, float(np.min((T - c_nd[pos]) / a_nd[pos])))
        if zero.size and np.any(c_nd[zero] > T + 1e-12):
            xl = max(xl, xh)
        xl = max(xl, 0.0)
        xh = min(xh, 1.0)

    if xl > xh + 1e-10:
        return h0

    if has_diag:
        Cq = T - c_diag + h0 - h0 * h0
        if Cq >= 0.25 - 1e-13:
            x = 0.5 * (xl + xh)
        else:
            disc = max(0.0, 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq)
            rt = np.sqrt(disc)
            r1 = 0.5 * (1.0 - rt)
            r2 = 0.5 * (1.0 + rt)
            x = 0.5 * (xl + xh)
            if x > r1 + 1e-12 and x < r2 - 1e-12:
                if abs(x - r1) <= abs(r2 - x):
                    x = r1
                else:
                    x = r2
            x = min(max(x, xl), xh)
    else:
        x = 0.5 * (xl + xh)

    return float(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0))


def coord_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng=None, nbisect=35):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    h_best = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_full(h)

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        cvec = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
        order = rng.permutation(n) if rng is not None else np.arange(n)
        improved = False

        for p in order:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            xnew = solve_coord_fast(cvec, h, p, n, nbisect)
            if abs(xnew - h[p]) > 1e-12:
                h[p] = xnew
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            break

        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        newc = compute_c5_full(h)

        if newc < best_c - 1e-16:
            best_c = newc
            h_best = h.copy()
        else:
            break
        h = h_best.copy()

    return h_best, best_c


def poly_half(N, a, kind):
    m = N // 2
    t = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / float(m)
    if kind == 1:
        c = (a - 1.0) / (2.0 * a)
        r = 1.0 - c
        x = c + r * np.power(t, a)
    else:
        c = (1.0 - a) / 2.0
        r = 1.0 - c
        x = 1.0 - r * np.power(1.0 - t, a)
    return project_half(x, m)


def resample_half(h, N):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) == N:
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_feasible(h, N)
        return project_half(h[:N // 2], N // 2)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
    xn = (np.arange(N) + 0.5) * (2.0 / N)
    hi = np.interp(xn, xo, h)
    hi = 0.5 * (hi + hi[::-1])
    hi = project_feasible(hi, N)
    return project_half(hi[:N // 2], N // 2)


def candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    N = 640
    cands = []

    for a in (0.72, 0.80, 0.86, 0.91, 0.96, 1.0):
        x = poly_half(N, a, 2)
        c = compute_c5_half(x, N)
        cands.append((c, x, N))

    for a in (1.0, 1.07, 1.15, 1.27, 1.42):
        x = poly_half(N, a, 1)
        c = compute_c5_half(x, N)
        cands.append((c, x, N))

    try:
        hp = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        hp = hp.reshape(-1)
        hp = project_feasible(hp, len(hp))
        hp = 0.5 * (hp + hp[::-1])
        hp = project_feasible(hp, len(hp))

        n0 = len(hp)
        if n0 % 2 == 0 and 160 <= n0 < 1000:
            x0 = project_half(hp[:n0 // 2], n0 // 2)
            c0 = compute_c5_half(x0, n0)
            cands.append((c0, x0, n0))

        xr = resample_half(hp, N)
        cr = compute_c5_half(xr, N)
        cands.append((cr, xr, N))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not cands:
        x = poly_half(N, 0.91, 2)
        cands.append((compute_c5_half(x, N), x, N))

    cands.sort(key=candidate_key)
    best_c, best_x, best_n = cands[0]

    for idx in range(min(5, len(cands))):
        if time.time() > deadline - 60:
            break
        c0, x0, n0 = cands[idx]
        if c0 >= best_c + 1e-9 and idx > 0:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 35:
            break
        sub_deadline = time.time() + min(130.0, max(25.0, 0.30 * rem * (N / float(n0))))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline - 45, sub_deadline)

        h0 = make_full(x0, n0)
        h1, c1 = coord_descent(h0, n0, 120, sub_deadline, rng, 32)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_n = n1 = n0
            best_x = project_half(h1[:n1 // 2], n1 // 2)

    if time.time() < deadline - 12:
        hf = make_full(best_x, best_n)
        hf, cf = coord_descent(hf, best_n, 2000, deadline - 4, rng, 38)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_c = cf
            best_x = project_half(hf[:best_n // 2], best_n // 2)

    if best_c > 0.37920 and time.time() < deadline - 150:
        N2 = min(960, int(round(best_n * 1.22)))
        if N2 % 2 == 0 and N2 > best_n + 32:
            hcur = make_full(best_x, best_n)
            x2 = resample_half(hcur, N2)
            h2 = make_full(x2, N2)
            h2, c2 = coord_descent(h2, N2, 80, min(deadline - 10, time.time() + 170), rng, 36)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_n = N2
                best_x = project_half(h2[:N2 // 2], N2 // 2)

    h_final = make_full(best_x, best_n)
    h_final = project_feasible(h_final, best_n)
    c_pre = compute_c5_full(h_final)

    h_sym = 0.5 * (h_final + h_final[::-1])
    h_sym = project_feasible(h_sym, best_n)
    c_sym = compute_c5_full(h_sym)

    if c_sym < c_pre:
        h_final, best_c = h_sym, c_sym
    else:
        best_c = c_pre

    return h_final, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
